\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

311 / 35 = 36

\n\n

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{(2023)^{2024} + (2023)^{2023} + 2024}{(2023)^{2023} + 1}\\) ਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ ਪਤਾ ਕਰਨਾ ਹੈ।

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾ:

\n\n

ਮੰਨ ਲਓ \\((2023)^{2023} = x\\)

\n\n

ਮੰਨ ਲਓ \\(x = (2023)^{2023}\\)।

\n\n

ਪਦ ਬਣ ਜਾਂਦਾ ਹੈ: \\(\\dfrac{(2023)\\cdot x + x + 2024}{x + 1}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{x(2023 + 1) + 2024}{x + 1}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{x(2024) + 2024}{x + 1}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{2024(x + 1)}{x + 1}\\)

\n\n

⇒ 2024

\n\n

∴ ਸਹੀ ਜਵਾਬ ਵਿਕਲਪ (4) ਹੈ।

Calculation:

\n\n

√(256) = 16

\n\n

√(0.0121) = 0.11

\n\n

√(4.41) = 2.1

\n\n

⇒ √(256) + √(0.0121) - √(4.41) = 16 + 0.11 - 2.1

\n\n

⇒ 16 + 0.11 - 2.1 = 14.01

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is option (4).

\n\n

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

\n\n

दिया गया है:

\n\n

\\(40^{-7} \\div (40^{19} \\times 40^{4})\\)

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

\\(a^m \\div a^n = a^{m-n}\\)

\n\n

\\(a^m \\times a^n = a^{m+n}\\)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

\\(40^{-7} \\div (40^{19} \\times 40^{4}) \\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(40^{-7} \\div 40^{(19+4)} \\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(40^{-7} \\div 40^{23} \\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(40^{-7 - 23} = 40^{-30} \\)

\n\n

∴ \\(40^{-7} \\div (40^{19} \\times 40^{4}) = 40^{-30} \\)

Given:

\n\n

Expression = (15.97)3 + 1.4373 × p + (0.03)3

\n\n

The expression must be a perfect cube.

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let a = 15.97, b = 0.03

\n\n

We know: (a + b)3 = a3 + b3 + 3ab(a + b)

\n\n

⇒ (15.97 + 0.03)3 = 16", "hn": "\n

दिया गया है:

\n\n

व्यंजक = (15.97)3 + 1.4373 x p + (0.03)3

\n\n

व्यंजक एक पूर्ण घन होना चाहिए।

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

माना कि a = 15.97, b = 0.03

\n\n

हम जानते हैं: (a + b)3 = a3 + b3 + 3ab(a + b)

\n\n

⇒ (15.97 + 0.03)3 = 163 = 4096

\n\n

अब गणना करें: a3 + b3

\n\n

⇒ (15.97)3 = 4073.003173

\n\n

⇒ (0.03)3 = 0.000027

\n\n

⇒ योग = 4073.003173 + 0.000027 = 4073.0032

\n\n

इसलिए, घन को पूरा करने के लिए शेष भाग = 4096 - 4073.0032 = 22.9968

\n\n

दिया गया मध्य पद 1.4373 × p है

\n\n

⇒ 1.4373 × p = 22.9968

\n\n

⇒ p = 22.9968 / 1.4373 = 16

\n\n

∴ व्यंजक को पूर्ण घन बनाने के लिए p = 16 का मान है।

\n\n

⇒ ਜੋੜ = 4073.003173 + 0.000027 = 4073.0032

\n\n

ਇਸ ਲਈ, ਘਣ ਨੂੰ ਪੂਰਾ ਕਰਨ ਲਈ ਬਾਕੀ ਬਚਿਆ ਹਿੱਸਾ = 4096 − 4073.0032 = 22.9968

\n\n

ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ ਵਿਚਕਾਰਲਾ ਪਦ 1.4373 x p ਹੈ

\n\n

⇒ 1.4373 x p = 22.9968

\n\n

⇒ p = 22.9968 / 1.4373 = 16

\n\n

∴ ਸਮੀਕਰਨ ਨੂੰ ਸੰਪੂਰਨ ਘਣ ਬਣਾਉਣ ਲਈ p = 16 ਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ।

Given:

\n\n

The expression is \\(\\sqrt{144} + \\sqrt{0.0225} - \\sqrt{9}\\).

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Evaluate each square root term individually:

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt{144} = 12\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt{0.0225}\\)

\n\n

⇒ To find \\(\\sqrt{0.0225}\\), consider \\(\\sqrt{225} = 15\\). Since there are four decimal places in 0.0225, there will be two decimal places in its square root.

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\sqrt{0.0225} = 0.15\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt{9} = 3\\)

\n\n

Substitute these values back into the expression:

\n\n

\\(12 + 0.15 - 3\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(12.15 - 3\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(9.15\\)

\n\n

∴ The value of the expression is 9.15.

Given:

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{64}{49}^{-\\dfrac{3}{2}} \\times \\dfrac{2}{7}^3 \\div \\sqrt[4]{(16)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

\\(a^{-n} = \\dfrac{1}{a^n}\\)

\n\n

\\((a^m)^n = a^{m \\times n}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt[n]{a} = a^{\\dfrac{1}{n}}\\)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Solving each term separately,

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{64}{49}^{-\\dfrac{3}{2}} = \\dfrac{1}{(\\dfrac{64}{49})^{\\dfrac{3}{2}}} = \\dfrac{1}{\\dfrac{8^3}{7^3}} = \\dfrac{7^3}{8^3} = \\dfrac{343}{512}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{2}{7}^3 = \\dfrac{8}{343}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt[4]{(16)^{-3}} = (16)^{-3/4} = \\dfrac{1}{16^{3/4}} = \\dfrac{1}{(2^4)^{3/4}} = \\dfrac{1}{2^3} = \\dfrac{1}{8}\\)

\n\n

Putting all the values in the expression:

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{64}{49}^{-\\dfrac{3}{2}} \\times \\dfrac{2}{7}^3 \\div \\sqrt[4]{(16)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{343}{512} \\times \\dfrac{8}{343} \\div \\dfrac{1}{8}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{343}{512} \\times \\dfrac{8}{343} \\times 8\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{343 \\times 8 \\times 8}{512 \\times 343} = \\dfrac{64}{512} = \\dfrac{1}{8}\\)

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is option (1).

\n\n

ఘాతాంకాల నియమం: \\(\\left(\\frac{a}{b}\\right)^{-n} = \\left(\\frac{b}{a}\\right)^n\\) లేదా \\(\\left(\\frac{1}{a}\\right)^{-n} = a^n\\)

\n\n

లెక్కలు:

\n\n

ప్రతి పదాన్ని ప్రతికూల ఘాతాంక నియమాన్ని ఉపయోగించి సరళీకరించండి:

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{1}{5}\\right)^{-2} = 5^2 = 25\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{1}{9}\\right)^{-2} = 9^2 = 81\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{1}{2}\\right)^{-2} = 2^2 = 4\\)

\n\n

ఇప్పుడు, సరళీకృత విలువలను జోడించండి:

\n\n

⇒ 25 + 81 + 4

\n\n

⇒ 106 + 4

\n\n

⇒ 110

\n\n

∴ ఇచ్చిన వ్యక్తీకరణ విలువ 110.

"1", "value": "\\(\\frac{1}{37}\\)"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "\\(\\frac{1}{41}\\)"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "\\(\\frac{1}{43}\\)"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "\\(\\frac{1}{36}\\)"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "4", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Given:

\n\n

Expression = \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\times \\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\div \\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

\\(a^{-n} = \\frac{1}{a^n}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{a}{b}\\right)^n = \\frac{a^n}{b^n}\\)

\n\n

\\((a^m)^n = a^{mn}\\)

\n\n

\\(a^{\\frac{m}{n}} = (\\sqrt[n]{a})^m\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt[n]{a} = a^{\\frac{1}{n}}\\)

\n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

Let's simplify each part of the expression.

\n\n

Part 1: \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\)

\n\n

\\(4096 = 64^2 = (8^2)^2 = 8^4 = (2^3)^4 = 2^{12}\\)दिया गया है:

\n\n

व्यंजक = \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\times \\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\div \\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

\\(a^{-n} = \\frac{1}{a^n}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{a}{b}\\right)^n = \\frac{a^n}{b^n}\\)

\n\n

\\((a^m)^n = a^{mn}\\)

\n\n

\\(a^{\\frac{m}{n}} = (\\sqrt[n]{a})^m\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt[n]{a} = a^{\\frac{1}{n}}\\)

\n\n

गणनाएँ:

\n\n

आइए व्यंजक के प्रत्येक भाग को सरल करें।

\n\n

भाग 1: \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\)

\n\n

\\(4096 = 64^2 = (8^2)^2 = 8^4 = (2^3)^4 = 2^{12}\\) (इसके अलावा, \\(4096 = 4^6 = (2^2)^6 = 2^{12}\\))

\n\n

\\(9 = 3^2\\)

\n\n

इसलिए, \\(\\left(\\frac{2^{12}}{3^2}\\right)^{-\\frac{3}{2}}\\) = \\(\\left(\\frac{3^2}{2^{12}}\\right)^{\\frac{3}{2}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{(3^2)^{\\frac{3}{2}}}{(2^{12})^{\\frac{3}{2}}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{3^{2 \\times \\frac{3}{2}}}{2^{12 \\times \\frac{3}{2}}} = \\dfrac{3^3}{2^{18}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{27}{2^{18}}\\)

\n\n

भाग 2: \\(\\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{2^2}{3}\\right)^5 = \\dfrac{(2^2)^5}{3^5} = \\dfrac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\)

\n\n

भाग 3: \\(\\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

\\(256 = 2^8\\)

\n\n

इसलिए, \\(\\sqrt[4]{(2^8)^{-3}}\\) = \\(\\sqrt[4]{2^{8 \\times (-3)}} = \\sqrt[4]{2^{-24}}\\)

\n\n

\\((2^{-24})^{\\frac{1}{4}}\\) = \\(2^{-24 \\times \\frac{1}{4}} = 2^{-6}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{1}{2^6}\\)

\n\n

सरलीकृत पदों को मिलाएँ:

\n\n

मूल व्यंजक भाग 1 x भाग 2 ÷ भाग 3 है

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{27}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\div \\left(\\frac{1}{2^6}\\right)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{27}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\times (2^6)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{3^3}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\times (2^6)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{3^3}{3^5} \\times \\dfrac{2^{10} \\times 2^6}{2^{18}}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{3-5} \\times 2^{10+6-18}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{-2} \\times 2^{16-18}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{-2} \\times 2^{-2}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{3^2} \\times \\dfrac{1}{2^2}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{9} \\times \\dfrac{1}{4}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\)

\n\n

इसलिए, दिए गए व्यंजक का मान \\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\) है।

ఇవ్వబడినది:

\n\n

వ్యక్తీకరణ = \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\times \\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\div \\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

\\(a^{-n} = \\frac{1}{a^n}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{a}{b}\\right)^n = \\frac{a^n}{b^n}\\)

\n\n

\\((a^m)^n = a^{mn}\\)

\n\n

\\(a^{\\frac{m}{n}} = (\\sqrt[n]{a})^m\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt[n]{a} = a^{\\frac{1}{n}}\\)

\n\n

గణనలు:

\n\n

వ్యక్తీకరణలోని ప్రతి భాగాన్ని సులభతరం చేద్దాం.

\n\n

భాగం 1: \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\)

\n\n

\\(4096 = 64^2 = (8^2)^2 = 8^4 = (2^3)^4 = 2^{12}\\) (అలాగే, \\(4096 = 4^6 = (2^2)^6 = 2^{12}\\))

\n\n

\\(9 = 3^2\\)

\n\n

కాబట్టి, \\(\\left(\\frac{2^{12}}{3^2}\\right)^{-\\frac{3}{2}}\\) = \\(\\left(\\frac{3^2}{2^{12}}\\right)^{\\frac{3}{2}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{(3^2)^{\\frac{3}{2}}}{(2^{12})^{\\frac{3}{2}}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{3^{2 \\times \\frac{3}{2}}}{2^{12 \\times \\frac{3}{2}}} = \\dfrac{3^3}{2^{18}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{27}{2^{18}}\\)

\n\n

భాగం 2: \\(\\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{2^2}{3}\\right)^5 = \\dfrac{(2^2)^5}{3^5} = \\dfrac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\)

\n\n

భాగం 3: \\(\\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

\\(256 = 2^8\\)

\n\n

కాబట్టి, \\(\\sqrt[4]{(2^8)^{-3}}\\) = \\(\\sqrt[4]{2^{8 \\times (-3)}} = \\sqrt[4]{2^{-24}}\\)

\n\n

\\((2^{-24})^{\\frac{1}{4}}\\) = \\(2^{-24 \\times \\frac{1}{4}} = 2^{-6}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{1}{2^6}\\)

\n\n

సరళీకరించిన పదాలను కలపండి:

\n\n

అసలు వ్యక్తీకరణ భాగం 1 x భాగం 2 ÷ భాగం 3

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{27}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\div \\left(\\frac{1}{2^6}\\right)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{27}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\times (2^6)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{3^3}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\times (2^6)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{3^3}{3^5} \\times \\dfrac{2^{10} \\times 2^6}{2^{18}}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{3-5} \\times 2^{10+6-18}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{-2} \\times 2^{16-18}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{-2} \\times 2^{-2}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{3^2} \\times \\dfrac{1}{2^2}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{9} \\times \\dfrac{1}{4}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\)

\n\n

∴ ఇచ్చిన వ్యక్తీకరణ విలువ \\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\).

दिले आहे:

\n\n

अभिव्यक्ती = \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\times \\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\div \\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

\\(a^{-n} = \\frac{1}{a^n}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{a}{b}\\right)^n = \\frac{a^n}{b^n}\\)

\n\n

\\((a^m)^n = a^{mn}\\)

\n\n

\\(a^{\\frac{m}{n}} = (\\sqrt[n]{a})^m\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt[n]{a} = a^{\\frac{1}{n}}\\)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

अभिव्यक्तीचा प्रत्येक भाग सोप्या पद्धतीने मांडूया.

\n\n

भाग 1: \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\)

\n\n

\\(4096 = 64^2 = (8^2)^2 = 8^4 = (2^3)^4 = 2^{12}\\) (तसेच, \\(4096 = 4^6 = (2^2)^6 = 2^{12}\\))

\n\n

\\(9 = 3^2\\)

\n\n

म्हणून, \\(\\left(\\frac{2^{12}}{3^2}\\right)^{-\\frac{3}{2}}\\) = \\(\\left(\\frac{3^2}{2^{12}}\\right)^{\\frac{3}{2}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{(3^2)^{\\frac{3}{2}}}{(2^{12})^{\\frac{3}{2}}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{3^{2 \\times \\frac{3}{2}}}{2^{12 \\times \\frac{3}{2}}} = \\dfrac{3^3}{2^{18}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{27}{2^{18}}\\)

\n\n

भाग 2: \\(\\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{2^2}{3}\\right)^5 = \\dfrac{(2^2)^5}{3^5} = \\dfrac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\)

\n\n

भाग 3: \\(\\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

\\(256 = 2^8\\)

\n\n

म्हणून, \\(\\sqrt[4]{(2^8)^{-3}}\\) = \\(\\sqrt[4]{2^{8 \\times (-3)}} = \\sqrt[4]{2^{-24}}\\)

\n\n

\\((2^{-24})^{\\frac{1}{4}}\\) = \\(2^{-24 \\times \\frac{1}{4}} = 2^{-6}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{1}{2^6}\\)

\n\n

सोप्या केलेल्या अटी एकत्र करा:

\n\n

मूळ अभिव्यक्ती भाग 1 x भाग 2 ÷ भाग 3 आहे

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{27}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\div \\left(\\frac{1}{2^6}\\right)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{27}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\times (2^6)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{3^3}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\times (2^6)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{3^3}{3^5} \\times \\dfrac{2^{10} \\times 2^6}{2^{18}}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{3-5} \\times 2^{10+6-18}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{-2} \\times 2^{16-18}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{-2} \\times 2^{-2}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{3^2} \\times \\dfrac{1}{2^2}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{9} \\times \\dfrac{1}{4}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\)

\n\n

∴ दिलेल्या अभिव्यक्तीचे मूल्य \\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\) आहे.

প্রদত্ত:

\n\n

রাশি = \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\times \\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\div \\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

ব্যবহৃত সূত্র:

\n\n

\\(a^{-n} = \\frac{1}{a^n}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{a}{b}\\right)^n = \\frac{a^n}{b^n}\\)

\n\n

\\((a^m)^n = a^{mn}\\)

\n\n

\\(a^{\\frac{m}{n}} = (\\sqrt[n]{a})^m\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt[n]{a} = a^{\\frac{1}{n}}\\)

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

রাশির প্রতিটি অংশকে সরল করা যাক।

\n\n

অংশ 1: \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\)

\n\n

\\(4096 = 64^2 = (8^2)^2 = 8^4 = (2^3)^4 = 2^{12}\\) (এছাড়াও, \\(4096 = 4^6 = (2^2)^6 = 2^{12}\\))

\n\n

\\(9 = 3^2\\)

\n\n

সুতরাং, \\(\\left(\\frac{2^{12}}{3^2}\\right)^{-\\frac{3}{2}}\\) = \\(\\left(\\frac{3^2}{2^{12}}\\right)^{\\frac{3}{2}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{(3^2)^{\\frac{3}{2}}}{(2^{12})^{\\frac{3}{2}}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{3^{2 \\times \\frac{3}{2}}}{2^{12 \\times \\frac{3}{2}}} = \\dfrac{3^3}{2^{18}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{27}{2^{18}}\\)

\n\n

অংশ 2: \\(\\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{2^2}{3}\\right)^5 = \\dfrac{(2^2)^5}{3^5} = \\dfrac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\)

\n\n

অংশ 3: \\(\\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

\\(256 = 2^8\\)

\n\n

সুতরাং, \\(\\sqrt[4]{(2^8)^{-3}}\\) = \\(\\sqrt[4]{2^{8 \\times (-3)}} = \\sqrt[4]{2^{-24}}\\)

\n\n

\\((2^{-24})^{\\frac{1}{4}}\\) = \\(2^{-24 \\times \\frac{1}{4}} = 2^{-6}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{1}{2^6}\\)

\n\n

সরলীকৃত পদগুলি একত্রিত করুন:

\n\n

মূল রাশিটি হল অংশ 1 × অংশ 2 ÷ অংশ 3

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{27}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\div \\left(\\frac{1}{2^6}\\right)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{27}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\times (2^6)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{3^3}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\times (2^6)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{3^3}{3^5} \\times \\dfrac{2^{10} \\times 2^6}{2^{18}}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{3-5} \\times 2^{10+6-18}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{-2} \\times 2^{16-18}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{-2} \\times 2^{-2}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{3^2} \\times \\dfrac{1}{2^2}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{9} \\times \\dfrac{1}{4}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\)

\n\n

∴ প্রদত্ত রাশির মান হল \\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\).

વપરાયેલ સૂત્ર:

\n\n

\\(a^{-n} = \\frac{1}{a^n}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{a}{b}\\right)^n = \\frac{a^n}{b^n}\\)

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ચાલો આપણે અભિવ્યક્તિના દરેક ભાગને સરળ બનાવીએ.

\n\n

\\(256 = 2^8\\)

\n\n

\n\n

\\((2^{-24})^{\\frac{1}{4}}\\) = \\(2^{-24 \\times \\frac{1}{4}} = 2^{-6}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{1}{2^6}\\)

\n\n

મૂળ અભિવ્યક્તિ ભાગ 1 x ભાગ 2 ÷ ભાગ 3 છે

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\)

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{3^2} \\times \\dfrac{1}{2^2}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{9} \\times \\dfrac{1}{4}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\)

\n\n

∴ ನೀಡಿರುವ ಪದದ ಮೌಲ್ಯವು \\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\) ಆಗಿದೆ.

\n\ned:Teea,\n

கொடுக்கப்பட்டது:

\n\n

சமன்பாடு = \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\times \\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\div \\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

பயன்படுத்தப்படும் சூத்திரம்:

\n\n

\\(a^{-n} = \\frac{1}{a^n}\\)

\n\n

கொடுக்கப்பட்டது:

\n\n

சமன்பாடு = \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\times \\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\div \\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

பயன்படுத்தப்படும் சூத்திரம்:

\n\n

\\(a^{-n} = \\frac{1}{a^n}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{a}{b}\\right)^n = \\frac{a^n}{b^n}\\)

\n\n

\\((a^m)^n = a^{mn}\\)

\n\n

\\(a^{\\frac{m}{n}} = (\\sqrt[n]{a})^m\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt[n]{a} = a^{\\frac{1}{n}}\\)

\n\n

கணக்கீடுகள்:

\n\n

சமன்பாட்டின் ஒவ்வொரு பகுதியையும் எளிதாக்குவோம்.

\n\n

பகுதி 1: \\(\\left(\\frac{4096}{9}\\right)^{-\\left(\\frac{3}{2}\\right)}\\)

\n\n

\\(4096 = 64^2 = (8^2)^2 = 8^4 = (2^3)^4 = 2^{12}\\) (மேலும், \\(4096 = 4^6 = (2^2)^6 = 2^{12}\\))

\n\n

\\(9 = 3^2\\)

\n\n

எனவே, \\(\\left(\\frac{2^{12}}{3^2}\\right)^{-\\frac{3}{2}}\\) = \\(\\left(\\frac{3^2}{2^{12}}\\right)^{\\frac{3}{2}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{(3^2)^{\\frac{3}{2}}}{(2^{12})^{\\frac{3}{2}}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{3^{2 \\times \\frac{3}{2}}}{2^{12 \\times \\frac{3}{2}}} = \\dfrac{3^3}{2^{18}}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{27}{2^{18}}\\)

\n\n

பகுதி 2: \\(\\left(\\frac{4}{3}\\right)^5\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{2^2}{3}\\right)^5 = \\dfrac{(2^2)^5}{3^5} = \\dfrac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\)

\n\n

பகுதி 3: \\(\\sqrt[4]{(256)^{-3}}\\)

\n\n

\\(256 = 2^8\\)

\n\n

எனவே, \\(\\sqrt[4]{(2^8)^{-3}}\\) = \\(\\sqrt[4]{2^{8 \\times (-3)}} = \\sqrt[4]{2^{-24}}\\)

\n\n

\\((2^{-24})^{\\frac{1}{4}}\\) = \\(2^{-24 \\times \\frac{1}{4}} = 2^{-6}\\) = \\(\\dfrac{1}{2^6}\\)

\n\n

எளிதாக்கப்பட்ட உறுப்புகளை இணைக்கவும்:

\n\n

கொடுக்கப்பட்ட சமன்பாடு பகுதி 1 x பகுதி 2 ÷ பகுதி 3

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{27}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\div \\left(\\frac{1}{2^6}\\right)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{27}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\times (2^6)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\left(\\frac{3^3}{2^{18}}\\right) \\times \\left(\\frac{2^{10}}{3^5}\\right) \\times (2^6)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{3^3}{3^5} \\times \\dfrac{2^{10} \\times 2^6}{2^{18}}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{3-5} \\times 2^{10+6-18}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{-2} \\times 2^{16-18}\\)

\n\n

\\(3^{-2} \\times 2^{-2}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{3^2} \\times \\dfrac{1}{2^2}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{9} \\times \\dfrac{1}{4}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\)

\n\n

∴ கொடுக்கப்பட்ட சமன்பாட்டின் மதிப்பு \\(\\dfrac{1}{36}\\) ஆகும்.

When dividing powers with the same base, subtract the exponents: (xm)/(xn) = x(m-n)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{a^4× b^3× c^4}{a^9× b^4× c^8}\\)

\n\n

⇒ a(4-9) × b(3-4) × c(4-8)

\n\n

⇒ a(-5) × b(-1) × c(-4)

\n\n

The simplified form is a(-5) × b(-1) × c(-4).

Given:

\n\n

40^0.11 = x

\n\n

40^0.1 = y

\n\n

x^z = y^2

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

From the property of exponents: a^(m*n) = (a^m)^n

\n\n

We use the equation x^z = y^2 and simplify using logarithms or exponent rules.

\n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

x = 40^0.11, y = 40^0.1

\n\n

Substitute values into the equation:

\n\n

(40^0.11)^z = (40^0.1)^2

\n\n

Using the property of exponents:

\n\n

40^(0.11*z) = 40^0.2

\n\n

Equating the powers of 40:

\n\n

0.11*z = 0.2

\n\n

z = 0.2 / 0.11

\n\n

z ≈ 1.82

\n\n

Therefore, the value of z is approximately 1.82.

\n\n

\n\n

Given:

\n\n

Expression: 50-3 ÷ 5015 × 50-18

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

For division: am ÷ an = am-n

\n\n

For multiplication: am × an = aदिया गया है:

\n\n

व्यंजक: 50-3 ÷ 5015 × 50-18

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

भाग के लिए: am ÷ an = am-n

\n\n

गुणन के लिए: am × an = am+n

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

चरण 1: भाग को सरल कीजिए:

\n\n

50-3 ÷ 5015 = 50-3-15 = 50-18

\n\n

चरण 2: परिणाम को 50-18 से गुणा कीजिए:

\n\n

50-18 × 50-18 = 50-18-18 = 50-36

\n\n

उत्तर: व्यंजक का मान 50-36 है, जो विकल्प 4 से मेल खाता है।

\n57:Tea1,\n

दिलेले आहे:

\n\n

पदावली: 50-3ఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

వ్యక్తీకరణ: 50-3 ÷ 5015 x 50-18

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

భాగహారానికి: am ÷ an = am-n

\n\n

గుణకారం కోసం: am x an = am+n

\n\n

లెక్క:

\n\n

దశ 1: భాగహారాన్ని సులభతరం చేయండి:

\n\n

50-3 ÷ 5015 = 50-3-15 = 50-18

\n\n

దశ 2: ఫలితాన్ని 50-18తో గుణించండి:

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\\(x^z = y^2\\) में प्रतिस्थापित करने पर:

\n\n

⇒ \\(z = \\frac{1.24}{0.42}\\)

\n\n

⇒ z = 2.95

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

தேவைப்படும் இடங்களில் அடுக்குகளை எளிதாக்க மடக்கைகளைப் பயன்படுத்துதல்.

\n\n

\\(26^{0.89} = x\\)

\n\n

\\(26^{0.31} = y\\)

\n\n

\\(x^z = y^2\\)

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Exponentiation property: \\(a^{m \\times n} = (a^m)^n\\)

\n\n

Taking logarithms to simplify exponents where necessary.

\n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

From the given:

\n\n

\\(x = 26^{0.89}\\) and \\(y = 26^{0.31}\\)

\n\n

Substituting into \\(x^z = y^2:\\)

\n\n

\\((26^{0.89})^z = (26^{0.31})^2\\)

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ \\(z = \\frac{0.62}{0.89}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(z \\approx 0.7\\)

\n\n

∴ The value of z is close to 0.7.

Given:

\n\n

420.66 = x, 420.65 = y, and xz = y4

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Express x and y in terms of 42:

\n\n

x = 420.66

\n\n

y = 420.65

\n\n

\n\n

(420.66)z = (420.65)4

\n\n

Simplify the exponents:

\n\n

420.66z = 422.6

\n\n

Equate the powers of 42:

\n\n

0.66z = 2.6

\n\n

Solve for z:

\n\n

z = 2.6 / 0.66

\n\n

z ≈ 3.94
